2026. 10.27
先週（10/19～10/26）発表の主な経済指標
米生産活動は堅調。AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子
製品や機械などがけん引。一方、データセンター建設に伴う需要
増加が、他産業の生産や建設活動を圧迫する兆候も
コア消費者物価は再び2％超え。特殊要因を除くサービス価格
も加速し、人件費転嫁の兆し。連合は2027年春闘で6％中心
を要求。中小企業の余力に課題も、物価上振れに注意
注目点② サービス物価の加速で重み増す2027年春闘戦線注目点①AI投資の恩恵拡大、副作用もじわり顕在化
総合PMIは57.6と高水準を維持。新規失業保険申
請件数も19.4万件と低水準で解雇の動きは引き続
き限定的。一方で、住宅着工件数や住宅建設業
者の景況感は悪化、高金利の影響が継続

ベージュブック
（2026/7）
米国：AI関連を中心に製造業生産は堅調も、資材・設備の需給ひっ迫が他産業に波及
 米国の製造業生産は、AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子製品、一次金属、一般機械などが堅調
― 一方、非耐久財や家具など消費関連の生産は軟調に推移し、業種間のばらつきが拡大
 FRBは、データセンター建設に伴う鋼材・電気設備などの需要増加が、他の建設案件や生産活動に影響していると指摘
―AI投資の拡大が関連産業の生産を押し上げる一方、供給制約を通じて他産業の活動を圧迫する兆候も
― 当面はAI投資による成長押し上げ効果が期待される一方、供給制約や価格上昇を通じた負の影響にも注意が必要
4

日本：全国コアCPIは再び2％超え。基調的なサービス価格も伸び高まる
5
 9月の全国コアCPIは前年比＋2.1％（前月：同＋1.7％）と加速。2025年12月以来の2％超に
― 原油高・円安等のコスト上昇もあり、家事用消耗品や教養娯楽用品等を中心に伸び率が拡大
― 一般サービス価格も伸び加速。シルバーウィークの並びによる宿泊料や外食費の上昇などが主因
 特殊要因を除いた一般サービス価格もじわり上昇
― 変動の大きい宿泊費や私立高校無償化による押し下げを除いた基調的な一般サービス価格に上昇圧力。人件費増の
価格転嫁が進んでいる可能性を示唆

全国コアCPI CPI一般サービス価格の推移

### Page 7

日本：連合は2027年春闘で賃上げ要求を強化。高水準の賃上げ持続と物価への波及が焦点
6
 連合は、2027年春闘賃上げ要求の目安を「6％中心、5％以上」と発表
―5％以上の賃上げを最低目標として明確化。実質賃金1％上昇の定着をめざす方針
― 中小企業は大企業との格差是正に向け「6％以上」が目標。過去4年のインフレで実質賃金が低下した分の回復も要求
― また、新たに、2030年に2026年の賃金水準を15％程度上回る中期目標を設定。持続的な賃金上昇をめざす
 賃上げ余力は企業規模・業種で濃淡。中小企業等で高い「賃上げ5％の壁」
― なお、6％に近い賃上げが実現する場合、サービス価格への波及が強まり、基調的物価上昇率が2％を上回る可能性
連合・春闘基本構想の新旧比較 春闘賃上げ率

基本構想」 より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 8

欧州：2027年度予算審議が本格化。生活苦によるデモが拡大する中、財政赤字は高水準
7
 2027年度予算審議が本格化。財政赤字は総じて高止まり
― 一定の防衛費・エネルギー関連支出を柔軟化する特例措置の適用後も、複数の国でEU財政基準で定める赤字幅を超
過。スペインは予算案を巡って議会解散。フランス・ベルギーも10/15時点でEUに予算案を未提出
 生活改善を求める抗議活動が欧州各国で発生。支出圧力の高まりは財政再建の障壁に
― フランスの学生によるデモを発端に、生活費の高騰や公共サービスの劣化に対する抗議活動は欧州各国に拡大。一部
は暴動に発展し、警察官と衝突する事態に
9～10月に発生した主な抗議活動主要国の財政収支実績と見通し
（出所） 各種報道より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

欧州：金融市場は政治・財政を巡る混乱を嫌気。不透明感は2027年入り後も継続する見込み
8
 金融市場は政治・財政を巡る混乱を警戒。主要国の選挙を控える中、不透明感は2027年まで継続へ
― 抗議活動が広がるイタリア、フランス、ベルギーの一般政府債務残高（GDP比）は、EU加盟国の中でも上位（それぞれ2
位、3位、4位）。金利上昇の背景は政治・財政への懸念
― 影響は為替市場にも波及。9月中旬以降、ユーロはドルに対して弱含み

26年1月 株価指数算出会社が株式保有構造の不透明さを問題視し、市場区分引き下げの可能性を警告

26年6月

の確認が必要と指摘。改善が不十分なら格下げを検討する
と警告

## Document 030: 2026-10-30_［QE予測］7～9月期実質GDPは年率＋1.0％とプラス成長を予想（1次QE）_4f5741cf

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-10-30_［QE予測］7～9月期実質GDPは年率＋1.0％とプラス成長を予想（1次QE）_4f5741cf.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

7～9 月期実質GDP は年率＋1.0％と 
プラス成長を予想（1 次QE）

○ 2026年7～9月期の実質GDP（1次速報値）は前期比＋0.2％（年率＋1.0％）と、4～6月期（前期比＋
0.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半期連続でのプラス成長を予想 
○ 個人消費が伸び悩む一方、設備投資や民間在庫が押し上げる見込み。なお、公的在庫と輸入では
中東情勢を主因とする一時的な変動が生じ、成長率にプラス・マイナス両面で影響 
○ 年度後半の日本経済は、小幅なプラス成長を予想。インフレ再加速を受け消費抑制が続く一方、設
備投資が堅調に拡大。日本銀行は12月会合で政策金利を1.50％に引き上げる見込み

7～9月期の実質GDP（1次
速報値）はプラス成長 を
予想 
11月16日 （月） に、 内閣府から2026年7～9月期の 「四半期別GDP速 報（QE）」
（1次速報値）が公表される。現時点で入手可能な基礎統計の結果を踏まえ
ると、7～9月期の実質GDPは前期比＋0.2％（ 年 率＋1.0％） となる見込みだ。
4～6月期の前期比＋0.4％（年率＋1.4％）からやや減速するものの、4四半
期連続でプラス成長になると予想する（図表）。 
7～9月期は、内需の柱である個人消費が伸び悩む一方、 設備投資の拡大や、
製造業を中心とする在庫積み増しが成長率を押し上げるとみられる。その他
では、前期の備蓄原油放出の反動が公的在庫投資のプラス要因として現れる
が、原油の代替調達進展を反映した輸入の増加がマイナス要因になるなど、

### Page 2

2

中東情勢による一時的な変動も引き続き成長率に影響する見込みだ。以下、
需要項目別に7～9月期の経済動向を詳しく確認しよう。 
個人消費は伸び悩み 。耐
久財の特需剥落や、 根強
い節約志向が影響 
個人消費は前期比▲0.1％（4～6月期：同＋0.0％） と、2四半期連続で伸び
悩む結果になろう。消費の弱さの背景には、 複合的な要因があると考えられ
る。 
耐久財では、自動車とエアコンの「特需」剥落が押し下げ要因になろう。
自動車は、購入時にかかる自動車税の環境性能割が3月末に廃止されたこと
を受け、4月から自動車販売が過去平均に比べ上振れていたが、8月以降は再
び過去平均並みの水準に戻っている。エアコンは、2027年4月の省エネ基準
引き上げで販売価格が上昇するとの見方から駆け込み需要が生じていたが、
景気ウォッチャー調査のコメントなどをみると、8月に入った後は一服した
模様だ。9月は気温が低かったほか、長雨による客足の減少もエアコンなど
季節家電の売り上げに悪影響を及ぼしたとみられる。 
食品やエネルギー、 各種消耗品などの非耐久財では、物価高を背景とする
根強い節約志向により、 弱含みが続いているようだ。今回のインフレ局面が
本格化する前の2020年と比較すると、非耐久消費財の物価は足元までの約6
年半でおよそ1.3倍の水準に上昇した計算になる。こうした累積的な価格上
昇が、 消費者の体感物価の高まりを通じて節約志向を強め、 購入数量の抑制
につながっている可能性がある。 
サービス消費は、 イベント関連支出などで底堅さがみられる一方、日常的
な支出には物価高による節約志向が波及し、 サービスや価格帯、 顧客層によ
る二極化が進みつつあるとみられる。 また、8～9月には台風・長雨による旅
行・ 宿泊のキャンセルや外出控えの影響から、 レジャー関連を中心に消費が
伸び悩んだようだ。 
設備投 資は3四半期ぶり
に前期比プラス 
設備投資は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.9％） と、3四半期ぶりに前期
比プラスへ転じると予想する。 
直近の設備投資関連指標をみると、資本財の国内向け供給量を表す資本財
総供給指数（除く輸送機械）が7～8月に大きく上昇した。また第3次産業活
動指数では、ソフトウェア業の実質売上高が同じく7～8月に増加したことが
確認でき、どちらも7～9月期の設備投資が拡大したことを示唆している。 
加えて、前期の4～6月期に発生した一時的な押し下げ圧力（特許権の海外
譲渡による国内研究開発投資の減少） がなくなったこともプラス要因だ。た
だし、 これは裏を返せば、7～9月期のQEにおける設備投資の伸びが実態以上
に強い数字になるということでもあり、評価する際には留意が必要である。 
住宅投資は 前期比マイナ
スが継続 
住宅投資は前期比▲0.8％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.6％ ）と、2四半期連続で前期
比マイナスとなろう。中東情勢を背景とする資材不足は徐々に緩和している
とみられるが、7～8月の住宅着工件数が伸び悩んでおり、住宅価格や金利上

### Page 3

3

昇による需要低迷に加えて、 足元では地震や台風などによる着工 ・ 進捗の遅
れが発生している可能性がある。 
民間 在庫は 2四半期連続
で成長率にプラス寄与 
民間在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度は、＋0.2％Pt（4～6月期：
＋0.3％Pt）とプラスが続く見込みだ。AI関連需要の強さなどを背景に、製
造業で在庫積み増しの動きが進んでいることが影響すると考えられる。な
お、4～6月期は石油化学製品などの供給不足を懸念した前倒し発注が在庫を
押し上げたとみられるが、7～9月期は原油の代替調達進展に伴って供給不足
が緩和したことで、そうした要因の影響は一巡した模様だ。 
公的需要 は備蓄原油放出
の反動で前期比プラスに 
公的需要は前期比＋1.2％（4～6月 期：同 ▲0.8％）と、2四半期ぶりの前期
比プラスを予想する。 
最大の要因は、公的在庫変動の実質GDP成長率に対する寄与度がプラスに
転じるとみられることだ。4～6月期は国家備蓄原油の放出により公的在庫変
動の寄与度が▲0.5％Ptと大幅マイナスになったが、7～9月期は国家原油備
蓄の減少が小幅なものにとどまり（6月末：105日分→9月末：102日分）、公
的在庫の減少ペースが緩和することで、成長率に対し＋0.3％Pt程度のプラ
ス寄与になると見込まれる。 
その他の公的需要項目では、政府消費を前期比＋0.2％（4～6月期：同＋
1.7％）と予想する。4～6月期は高校授業料・小学校給食費の無償化開始に
より高い伸びになったが、7～9月期はそうした特殊要因が剥落し、緩やかな
増加ペースに戻ると考えられる。公共投資は前期比▲0.5％（4～6月期：同
▲0.5％）と、土木関連工事の減少を受けて2四半期連続のマイナスを見込む。 
輸出が小幅に減少する 一
方、 輸入が増加し、外需は
4四半期ぶりのマイナス
寄与 
外需では、輸出が前期比▲0.3％（4～6月期：同＋0.4％）と小幅に減少す
る一方、輸 入が同＋1.3％（4～6月 期：同▲1.7％）と増加に転じる見込みだ。
実質GDP成長率に対する外需の寄与度は▲0.3％Ptと、4四半期ぶりのマイナ
スが予想される。 
輸入の増加は、原油の代替調達進展が主因だ。中東情勢の緊迫化により4
～6月期は一時的に原油輸入が急減したが、7～9月期は米国産原油やホルム
ズ海峡を通過しない中東産原油の調達が増え、9月を除いて前年並みの輸入
量が確保されている。 
輸入はGDPの控除項目であることから、7～9月期は輸入増加が成長率の下
押し要因になる。 ただし、代替調達の進展に伴って国家備蓄原油の放出が抑
制され、 先述したように7～9月期は公的在庫変動が成長率にプラス寄与する
とみられることから、輸入増による成長率押し下げ効果の一部は公的在庫変
動により相殺されるだろう。 
輸出では、前期の4～6月期に特許権の海外譲渡がサービス輸出を押し上げ
た （これは4～6月期の設備投資の押し下げ要因でもある） 反動が現れ、 一時
的に下振れするだろう。一方、旺盛なAI関連需要が支えになり、半導体・電

### Page 4

4

子部品や半導体製造装置などの財輸出は堅調に推移する見込みだ。インバウ
ンド需要 （非居住者家計の国内での直接購入） は、 日中対立の継続による中
国人訪日客の少なさに加え、 航空運賃上昇を受けた欧米訪日客の伸び悩みに
より、低調な結果になると見込まれる。 
2026 年度後半 の実質 GDP
は小幅プラス と予想 。個
人消費が伸び悩む一方、
設備投資が堅調拡大 
以上のとおり、7～9月期の日本経済は前期から減速するものの、4四半期
連続でプラス成長になると予想する。GDPの約半分を占める個人消費の伸び
悩みが足を引っ張っているが、設備投資や製造業を中心とする在庫積み増し
がけん引する格好だ。 
先行きについてみると、2026年度後半（2026年10～2027年3月）の 実質GDP
は、 均してみれば小幅なプラス成長になると予想する。個人消費が振るわな
い一方で、 設備投資の拡大が支えになる構図が続くだろう。2026年度全体の
実質GDP成長率は、前年度末の堅調な伸びで今年度の平均水準が押し上げら
れることもあり（いわゆるプラスのゲタ）、前年比＋1％程度とまずまずの
成長率になる計算だ。 
年度後半の個人消費では、インフレ再加速による実質賃金の伸び悩みが消
費拡大の抑制要因になるだろう。 原油 ・ ナフサ価格高騰の影響が徐々に波及
し、 コア消費者物価指数 （生鮮食品を除く総合） は年度末にかけて前年比＋
3％程度まで上昇すると予想される（ただし、2026年末から政府の電気・ガ
ス料金支援策が実施されれば、0.4～0.5％Pt程度の押し下げ要因になる可能
性がある）。加えて、2027年4月の消費税減税開始前には、保存食品などを
中心とした買い控えの動きが生じるとみられ、 一時的に消費が落ち込む可能
性がある。 
他方で、年度後半の設備投資は堅調に拡大するだろう。日銀短観（9月調
査） では、2026年度の設備投資計画 （ソフトウェア・研究開発を含む設備投
資額 （除く土地投資額）） が前年比＋9.3％と高い伸びを維持した （6月 調 査：
同＋8.8％）。生産・営業用設備投資判断DIが2025年度後半以降に低下し、
設備の不足感が強まっていることをあわせて考えれば、 設備投資は今後拡大
傾向で推移していく見込みだ。 
また、政府の投資支援策である「大胆な投資促進税制」 （特定生産性向上
設備等投資促進税制）が2026年7月末から開始されたことも、今後の設備投
資にとってプラス要因になろう。同税制における減税規模の政府見込みを参
考に試算すると、2026～2028年度に設備投資の前年比変化率を約0.1％Ptず
つ押し上げる効果があると考えられる。 
ただし、 日銀が来年にかけて政策金利を一段と引き上げ、 企業の新規借入
金利がさらに上昇すると予想されること、 また、 投資財の価格が上昇傾向を
続けると考えられることを踏まえると、 中小企業を中心に設備投資を控える
企業が今後増加する可能性がある点には注意が必要だ。

### Page 5

5

日銀は10月会合で 政策金
利を据え置き 。次回利上
げは12月と予想 
10月29・30日に開催された金融政策決定会合において、 日本銀行は政策金
利を現行の1.25％で据え置いた。次の利上げ（1.25％→1.50％） は、次回12
月17・18日の会合と予想する。 前回利上げを行った9月会合に続いて 「3カ月
に1回」ペースでの利上げになり、 日銀の利上げペースが6月以前の 「半年強
に1回」から加速していることを印象付けるものになろう。 
企業の価格転嫁行動は一段と積極化しており、植田総裁が9月会合後の記
者会見で 「政策の局面が変化した」と説明したように、日本銀行は「物価引
き上げ」 から 「物価上振れ抑制」 へと金融政策運営の舵を切っている。12月
の追加利上げ後も、 金利上昇の影響を確認しながら、 日銀は最終的に2027年
半ばにかけて政策金利を2.0％まで引き上げていくだろう。 
なお、経済見通しの詳細については、みずほ総合研究所調査部「2026年・
2027年 みずほ世界経済見通し ─ 転換点は近いのか、投資主導経済の行方
を探る ─」（10月2日）をご覧いただきたい。 
（https://www.mizuhobank.co.jp/corporate/mhri/research/report/pdf/outlook261002.pdf）

Cathay’s View市場策略思維
市場焦點
1. 市場焦點:
• 重要企業公布財報, 牽動AI類股走勢: Anthropic發布IPO公開說明書, CSP與記憶體大廠陸續公布財報,
個股走勢分化。
• 央行按下暫停鍵, 但不代表升息週期結束: 美、日、歐央行均選擇按兵不動, 但12月升息機率仍偏高。
• 殖利率快速回落, 創2023以來單日最大跌幅: AI融資疑慮再次發酵, 實質利率回落使債券久違重拾避險
資產角色。
2. 後續看點:
• 11/2美國製造業PMI、11/3-11/4美國期中選舉、11/4美國服務業PMI、11/6美國非農與失業率
策略思維
1. 股市−選舉前波動易放大, 但長多趨勢尚未結束: 就歷史數據而言, 美國期中選舉前股市表現往往較差, 但AI
需求仍未見終點, 強勁基本面支撐下股市仍有高點可期。
2. 債市−殖利率飆升暫緩, 但高盤格局不易改變: 近期債券暫時重拾避險資產角色使殖利率明顯回落, 但AI相關
企業發債量攀升的趨勢仍在, 且通膨快速回落不易, 預期殖利率仍偏向高檔震盪。

3. 匯市−美元指數漲勢暫緩, 但強美元仍將維持一段時間: 雖近期美元指數漲勢趨緩, 但Warsh決心抗通膨並重
建聯準會獨立性, 加上歐洲動盪未平, 美元短期內轉弱難度偏高。

Anthropic營收成長驚人, 預估Q3營收超過上半年總和 Anthropic未來算力採購計畫
資料來源: Anthropic預估, 預估期間為2026Q3, 截至2026/10/26。 資料來源: Anthropic, 截至2026/10/26。
• Anthropic IPO公開說明書指出, 今年Q2營收116億美元, 年增近14倍, 季增144%, 並預估Q3營收168億美元, 年增
11倍, 季增45%, 超過今年上半年營收總和, 營收成長驚人。此外, Q3營收是同期算力支出的兩倍, 預估Q4營收是
算力支出的1.7倍, 展現公司強勁的AI營收變現能力。
• Anthropic亦定調公司核心策略, 未來將擴大投入, 透過提升模型、降低token成本, 帶動客戶使用量增加, 進而帶動
營收成長, 並再度擴大投資。公司預估至2036年, 將投入約8000億美元採購算力, 為近一年投資的50倍, 反映AI需
求展望強勁。

主要雲端服務商財報狀況
註: 基於公平比較, EPS指GAAP EPS數值。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 國泰世華銀行整理, 截至2026Q3財報。
• 隨著大型 CSP 業者陸續公布財報 , 除了企業獲利表現 , 自由現金流是否穩定或改善 , 也成為市場關注的焦點 。
SpaceX由虧轉盈, 自由現金流也大幅改善, 帶動股價於盤後大漲7.8%。微軟的自由現金流雖小幅下滑, 但仍穩定
維持在百億美元水準, 盤後股價同樣上漲。反觀Meta、谷歌與亞馬遜, 則因自由現金流惡化, 導致盤後股價重挫。
• 不過, 無論各家業者的股價表現如何分歧, 其共同點都是肯定AI需求依然強勁, 且已實質反映在營收成長上, 同時持
續擴大資本支出。其中, SpaceX雲端收入成長8倍, 谷歌雲端收入成長102%, 微軟Azure成長48%,亞馬遜AWS年增
39%, 均展現強勁的成長動能。

台股指數創新高, 但獲利成長更快, 估值反而下降 台積電資本支出快速上升
資料來源: Bloomberg預估, 截至2026/10/30。 資料來源: 台積電, JP預估, 截至2026/10/30。
• 台股指數雖拉回, 但企業獲利持續走高, 使台股本益比進一步降至近期低檔, 顯示AI投資造成資金吃緊、殖利率走
揚的現象, 對評價壓力加大。這也使得高本益比的族群拉回時壓力更大, 企業獲利成長性較佳的類股具下檔支撐。
• 台積電對於AI長期成長看法未變, 在持續擴大資本支出時也深入評估客戶端風險 , 由於未來3年台積電資本投資仍
將遠高於過去3年, 預料明年缺貨現象仍將普遍出現, 考量AI需求與企業獲利趨勢延續, 台股拉回仍可留意布局機會。

SK hynix獲利持續創新高, 營利率維持高檔 SK hynix財報會議重點
韓股｜匯率逆風下財報仍超預期, SK hynix重申樂觀展望
• SK hynix公布26Q3財報, 營業利潤83.3兆韓元、營業利潤率79.6%均創歷史新高, 均優於市場預期。儘管淨利因匯
兌與業外投資損失而不及預期, 但核心業務表現強勁, 股價大漲10.9%。
• 管理層正面釐清多項原先市場擔憂的議題, 並重申AI需求仍然極為強勁, 明後兩年供不應求的狀況將持續。雖從獲
利年增率的角度而言, 2027年很難優於2026年, 但回顧過去記憶體類股的歷史週期, 只要獲利能夠持續增長而不衰
退, 股價仍然存在上行空間。

記者會重點一覽10月維持利率不變, 2Y仍反應升息預期
資料來源: Bloomberg, 2026/10/29。 資料來源: Fed, 國泰世華銀行整理。
10月FOMC｜觀望9月緊縮效果, 重申捍衛通膨決心
10月FOMC (10/28)
經濟
• 經濟強勁擴張: 預估第三季成長3%以上, 資本支出佔
GDP比重處於歷史高位, 由科技領域逐步擴張。經濟
擴張源於供給側生產力, 而非總體需求過熱。
通膨
• 捍衛2%PCE目標: 通膨已高於目標近 6年, Warsh強
調Fed負有絕對責任, 無軟性通膨目標。
• 油價推升短期通膨預期, 中長期Swaps依然錨定。
升息
傳導效果
• 9月升息後, 金融與信用情勢已如預期緊縮。
• 高利率穿透力 : 高科技產業的信用利差已出現擴大 ,
貨幣緊縮對前沿科技資本形成融資約束力。
政策展望
• 通膨控管並非綁定單一數據, 動態評估最佳政策立場。
• 會議調整: 未來維持每年 8次FOMC會議, 惟2027年
調整一次為「戰略研討」, 2028年新增至兩次。

10年債利率跌回5%下方, 惟期限溢價仍高 TIPS回落, Breakeven相對平穩
資料來源: Bloomberg, ACM為紐約聯準會之期限溢價預估模型, 截至2026/10/27。
註:名目利率 ≈ 實質利率(TIPS) + 通膨預期(Breakeven Rate)。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/27。
• 市場對AI投資回收進度與獲利轉換能力產生疑慮, 帶動全球科技股修正; 資金流向避險資產, 帶動美國10年期公債
殖利率於10/22單日下跌16.6bps, 目前於5%附近震盪。
• 從利率結構觀察, 10年Breakeven Rate大致維持在2.3%: 呼應主席Warsh所言, 油價拉高民眾「短期通膨預期」,
但中長期通膨指標依然錨定。相較之下, 10年TIPS持續攀升為Fed升息帶動實質利率走升、信用情勢收緊。
• 此外, 美國財政紀律、美國公債供給增加、AI Capex帶動企業發債需求, 仍將支撐期限溢價處於高檔; 雖短線長端

利率受買盤影響回落, 但結構性因素驅使長端利率中樞位居高位的情勢未改。

註: 公司估值上升, 使融資工具價值增加, 因此需認列帳面損失, 但不造成實質現金流出。
資料來源: Anthropic, 截至2026/6/30。
註: Non-GAAP營業利益率 = (GAAP營業利益-金融負債公允價值損益調整)/營收。
資料來源: Anthropic, 截至2026/6/30。
• Anthropic Q2 GAAP淨利為-663億美元, 但主因是隨估值增加, 公司融資工具帳面價值同步增加, 形成帳面損失, 但
不造成實際支出。排除會計評價損益調整後, 2026上半年non-GAAP淨利達27億美元, 獲利已轉正。
• 此外, Q2 non-GAAP營利率達19%, 預估Q3亦能維持正向, 反映公司獲利能力已有一定基礎, 未來可望持續加速。
未來投資人可關注Anthropic營收增速是否保持高速成長, 以及non-GAAP營利率與淨利是否持續改善, 作為公司

AI投資變現能力與算力需求前景觀察指標。

註: OpenAI、Anthropic參考價取期貨價格。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/28。
• 日經225指數今年高點出現在6/22, 與費城半導體指數同天見高。日股回檔4個月以來, 走勢與費半高度一致, 除了
日股的半導體設備股居產業領導地位外, 鎧俠搭上記憶體大漲行情, 以及軟銀大舉投資參與AI行情, 也使得日股科
技佔比雖不像台、韓股市那麼高, 但走勢也與半導體走勢、AI行情密不可分。
• 軟銀投資OpenAI 13%股權, 並持有Arm 86%股份, 就是看好AI成長題材, 未來在OpenAI上市後能享受增值利益, 雖
然OpenAI取消2026年IPO計劃, 軟銀股價自6月以來也幾乎腰斬, 不過隨著Anthropic計劃於11月中下旬完成掛牌,
估值約1.5~2兆美元, 預料將激勵OpenAI估值, 也有望拉抬軟銀股價。

日本央行兩名鷹派委員(田村、高田)明年任期屆滿, 鴿派話語權料將上升
日圓｜正面與負面催化劑並存, 日圓料將維持區間震盪格局
• 日本央行貨幣政策正常化速度仍然偏慢, 且明年將有兩位鷹派委員卸任, 預期後續升息幅度與速度均受到制約。美
日利差快速縮窄不易, 使目前日圓尚不具備大幅升值條件。
• 然而, 近期日本官方對於160價位的防守意願強烈, 頻頻透過口頭干預止貶。同時日債殖利率上行, 使國內退休基金
與壽險具備資金回流本國資產誘因, 使日圓貶幅亦有支撐。多空因素疊加下, 預期日圓至年底仍應維持區間走勢。

通膨上行風險仍存 升息預期較9月會議有所降溫, 市場預估未來2~3碼空間
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/9。
註: 比較與9月會議之升息預期差異。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/29。
• 歐洲央行(ECB)如預期維持三大利率不變 (DF/MRO/MLF分別維持在2.50%/2.65%/2.90%)。隨能源價格上漲推升
通膨上行風險與經濟下行壓力 ; 目前政策關鍵在於管控通膨風險 , 同時評估能源衝擊對實體經濟的影響性 。此外,
總裁Lagarde宣布將於明年2月提前卸任, 增添繼任角力與政策不確定性。
• 儘管面臨領導層異動, 仍預計ECB維持偏鷹政策立場: 10月聲明中強調基本通膨(Underlying inflation)大致維持穩定,
且薪資成長未顯著走升; 但若能源衝擊規模持續擴大, 仍需觀望是否產生二輪通膨效應。
• 隨美伊局勢持續膠著, 全球原油運輸與供應鏈不確定性高企。在通膨風險未完全消退下, 預計ECB將維持數據導向

與漸進升息的彈性空間; 目前期貨市場仍定價未來一年具2~3碼的升息空間, 相較9月會議略有降溫。

國家隊進出狀況 A股指數與評價走勢
中國｜外需撐起GDP, 政治邏輯重塑定價, 大型政策刺激暫且無望, 指數需時間築底
• Q3 GDP年增4.5%, 較上季4.3%回升, 前三季累計達4.6%, 落全年4.5~5%目標區間, 「穩中有升」反而讓政策刺激
期待落空。GDP結構並不均衡, 出口與高端製造良好; 但民間消費僅溫和擴張、投資持續下滑, 房地產依舊拖累。
• 另方面, 人民日報先以「全面從嚴治黨」為五中全會定調。將長征、遵義會議、毛澤東核心確立, 串聯至「兩個確
立」與「十五五」規劃。黨敘事把組織置於發展之上, 呼應「管黨治黨越有效, 經濟發展的保障越有力」的主張。
• 考量數據與治理取向, 1)短線行情偏淡、指數需時間築底, 因為大規模刺激期待落空, 加上國際氣氛讓AI硬體股跟

跌, 資金只能轉向金融股暫待; 2)中長線在黨建先於發展的邏輯下, 資金還是只能流向國企色彩高、與政策對齊如
半導體、能源安全、軍工等類股。

魯拉的政策方向對於財政負擔明顯較重 市場提前定價魯拉下台, 選情逆轉後股匯雙殺
巴西｜現任總統魯拉意外逆轉勝, 財政可持續性疑慮使巴西面臨股匯雙殺
• 巴西現任總統魯拉於第二輪投票中逆轉勝, 出乎市場預期。由於魯拉的政策將使巴西面臨較大的財政風險, 且原先
市場早已定價小波索納洛(其父2022敗選後被控政變罪, 遭判處監禁27年)將勝出, 故結果出爐後, 巴西的股匯市大
幅下跌。
• 根據最新消息, 小波索納洛拒絕承認選舉結果, 同時無視限制出境令前往美國尋求庇護。考量原先魯拉與川普關係
不睦, 不排除近期美國再對巴西祭出制裁措施, 美巴雙邊風險上升或將使近期巴西金融市場持續動盪。
資料來源: Bloomberg, 國泰世華銀行整理, 截至2026/10/28。 資料來源: Bloomberg, 截至2026/10/28。

Powerchip Semiconductor Manufacturing Co  | Asia
Pacific
Margin beat; pricing catch-up
continues
3Q26 earnings beat:
• Revenue was NT$21.34bn, +23% Q/Q, +80% Y/Y, 2% below our estimate.
• Gross profit was NT$10.1bn, GM was 47.5%, +19.3ppt Q/Q, +54.1ppt Y/Y,
21.2ppt above our estimate
• EPS was NT$1.38, +82% Q/Q, 84% above our estimate.
• 3Q26 recorded NT$660mn of opex associated with the Tongluo fab
(reclassified from COGS).
Other takeaways:

• AI packaging remains an execution watchpoint – 3D AI foundry's revenue
decreased from 5.4% of the mix in 2Q to 1.4% in 3Q during the relocation,
although interposers are expected to be fully loaded by year-end.
• IPD offers structural growth potential. Management estimates that  industry-
wide 30-50kwpm high capacitance demand for 2H27 and  onward far exceeds
PSMC's planned capacity of 8-10kwpm.
• PWF mass production is targeted for 4Q27, with revenue growth

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Powerchip Semiconductor Manufacturing Co (6770.TW)
Base case, 2.0x 2027e P/B. This method is in line with our approach for Greater China memory
IDM players, in view of the industry's high volatility. Our 2.0x target P/B is higher than the
historical average of 1.5x since 2021, reflecting our optimistic view on improved pricing and
utilization.
Risks to Upside
n Pricing power is sustained in 2026.
n Listing on main board attracts investment fund flows.

n Aluminum process enjoys higher margins.
Risks to Downside
n Competition from China intensifies.
n Price erosion is faster than expected.
n Demand from consumer electronics clients weakens further.
n Inventory in the supply chain takes longer to digest.

Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 116: 261027_daiwa_unimicron

Source PDF: all report pdf/261027_daiwa_unimicron.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Our first take on Unimicron’s 3Q26 results (released on 27 October) is that 
earnings were better than expected, driven by stronger-than-expected non-operating 
gains. We attribute the upside primarily to a smaller-than-feared valuation loss on UMC in 
3Q26. In addition, the company mentioned that other investments, including Unimicron 
shares held through investment vehicles, contributed c.TWD3bn of mark-to-market gains in 
3Q26. As for the core business, 3Q26 revenue reached TWD52.7bn (up 23% QoQ, up 
55% YoY), with gross margin expanding 4.0ppt QoQ to 28.8% and operating margin rising 
4.5ppt QoQ to 20.0%, supported by improving profitability in ABF substrates and HDI. We 
believe the profitability performance was broadly in line with the latest buy-side 
expectations. Looking ahead, while the company does not provide numerical guidance, 
revenue and gross margin should continue to improve in 4Q26. Given Unimicron’s leading 
position across most CSP AI ASIC projects, Nvidia GPUs, and EMIB-T, we believe the 
company should continue to benefit from a favourable pricing environment amid the 
current supply-demand dynamics and ongoing specification upgrades, including larger 
substrate body sizes and technology migration. We have a Buy (1) rating on Unimicron 
with a 12M TP of TWD1,500, based on a target PER of 35x applied to our 1-year-forward 
EPS forecast. Key downside risk: weaker-than-expected demand.

Unimicron’s 3Q26 results highlights  
(TWDm) Actual QoQ% YoY% Daiwa Diff % Consensus Diff % 
Revenue 52,650 23% 55% 51,251 3% 50,366 5% 
Gross profit 15,189 43% 234% 14,513 5% 14,044 8% 
  Gross margin (%) 28.8%   28.3%  27.9%  
Operating profit 10,528 58% 587% 9,901 6% 9,878 7% 
  Operating margin (%) 20.0%   19.3%  19.6%  
Pre-tax profit 13,163 -15% 347% 5,473 141% 8,577 53% 
Net profit 10,052 -23% 358% 4,184 140% 6,738 49% 
EPS (TWD) 6.15 -23% 358% 2.72 140% 4.12 49% 
Source: Company data, Daiwa forecasts, Bloomberg

What’s the impact: 
Below is our first take on Unimicron’s 3Q26 results (released on 27 October). We will 
review our model in due course.

• 3Q26 results beat expectations. Unimicron’s 3Q26 earnings came in at TWD10.1bn 
(EPS: TWD6.15), beating our estimate of TWD4.2bn and Bloomberg consensus of 
TWD6.7bn by 140% and 49%, respectively, mainly due to higher-than-expected non-
operating gains. In our previous note, Unimicron recognised c.TWD8bn of mark-to-
market gains on UMC (2303 TT, TWD122.5, Neutral [3], covered by Rick Hsu) in 2Q26. 
We had originally expected Unimicron to recognise c.TWD5bn of valuation losses in 
3Q26, given UMC’s share-price performance in July. However, the actual valuation loss 
of c.TWD0.7bn was substantially better than feared. In addition, the company attributed 
part of the strength to c.TWD3bn of other mark-to-market gains, including gains related 
to Unimicron shares held through its investment vehicles. This is broadly consistent with 
the reported 3Q26 total non-operating contribution of c.TWD2.6bn after accounting for 
the UMC valuation loss and other non-operating items.

### Page 2

Unimicron Technology Corp (3037 TT) 
27 October 2026 
2

• Looking at the core business, 3Q26 revenue reached TWD52.7bn (up 23% QoQ, up 
55% YoY), with gross margin expanding 4.0ppt QoQ to 28.8% and operating margin 
rising 4.5ppt QoQ to 20.0%, supported by improving profitability in ABF substrates and 
HDI. With stronger operating leverage, operating profit surged 58% QoQ to 
TWD10.53bn. We believe the gross-margin and operating-margin performance was 
broadly in line with recent buy-side expectations for modestly improving profitability in 
ABF substrates and HDI, given that ABF margin was already high at c.40% in 2Q26, 
alongside a higher revenue contribution from a large end customer. We estimate ABF 
substrate margin at c.42-44% in 3Q26 (vs. c.40% in 2Q26).

• Business outlook. While the company does not provide numerical guidance, revenue 
and gross margin should continue to improve in 4Q26 amid the current substrate supply 
shortage. In addition, the company is aggressively expanding capacity at its Guangfu 
and Yangmei plants over 2027-29, with roughly c.30% annual ABF capacity expansion, 
which should become a key growth driver for Unimicron. Given Unimicron’s leading 
position across most CSP AI ASIC projects, Nvidia GPUs, and EMIB-T, we believe the 
company should continue to benefit from a favourable pricing environment amid the 
current supply-demand dynamics and ongoing specification upgrades, including larger 
substrate body sizes and technology migration.

What we recommend: 
We have a Buy (1) rating on Unimicron with a 12M TP of TWD1,500, based on a target 
PER of 35x applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key downside risk: less-than-
expected demand.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): New AI server racks and Foldable phones to drive 4Q26E

We expect Hon Hai’s monthly revenues to continue sequential growth through Dec 
2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +39% QoQ, driven by: (1) AI data center: The 
next-generation AI server racks and CPO switches ramp-up, with higher ASP and early 
delivery. We expect Hon Hai to be the first to deliver the latest model, around 2-3 
months ahead of peers, driving its Oct and onward revenue growth. Followed by 
GPU-powered AI server racks, the LPU-powered AI server racks should fuel the growth 
in Dec and onward, supporting the YoY increase in 1Q27. As our Global Server TAM 
update shows, we expect the AI server rack global value TAM to grow at 163% CAGR in 
2025-28E to US$1tn, or 63% of the global server value TAM by 2028E. And (2) 
Smartphone: riding on the major brand customer’s latest model launches along with the 
new form factor to attract more end users. As we highlighted in September (report link), 
we reduced our global smartphone volume estimates by 1m/ 8m/ 0.2m units to 1.1bn / 
1.2bn/ 1.2bn in 2026-28E, or -10% / +2%/ +2% YoY on high memory costs; however, we 
remain positive on the potential for global leading brands to outperform the market 
given their strong brand awareness, large procurement power, and customers’ stronger 
consumption power. We remain positive on foldable phones and expect premium 
models to continue to gain market share, which should be positive to Hon Hai. Maintain 
Buy (on CL).

3-month revenues preview: We expect the company’s 4Q26E revenues to be +61% 
YoY/ +39% QoQ to NT$4.2 trillion. 3Q26 delivered strong revenues YoY growth of 47%, 
or 5% / 7% ahead of our / Bloomberg consensus, and we expect to see both YoY and 
MoM growth throughout Oct-Dec, driven by new AI server racks, new smartphone 
models, and CPO switches. Management noted that AI-related products remain on track 
to maintain solid growth momentum through 4Q26, while ICT products continue to 
benefit from typical 2H seasonal tailwinds, which should support overall operational 
performance in line with current consensus expectations.

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.  
Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI servers ramp-up to drive growth ahead; model transition and PC 
shipment slowdown could weigh on 4Q26

Quanta Sep revenues declined by 23% MoM, which we attributed to AI servers model 
transition, while PC entered quarter-end at a strong MoM of +62% MoM. Looking 
forward, we expect monthly revenues to resume sequential growth in Oct to Dec, riding 
on a reducing impact of AI servers model transition as new racks ramp up, along with 
growing general servers on agentic AI, and ASP increase on better specification and high 
raw material costs. We expect 4Q26E revenues to decline by 4% QoQ, considering the 
high base in 3Q26 and the gradual ramp-up of new AI server racks. As we highlighted in 
Oct (Global Server TAM updates), we expect global AI server racks (powered by NVIDIA 
and AMD) shipment at 63k / 110k / 164k in 2026E-28E along with the specification 
upgrade to drive the ASP; we raised leading US CSPs aggregate capex by 4% / 17% / 
22% in 2026E-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E, reflecting our positive view on AI spending.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$300 is based on a 
10.7x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. Key 
upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers. 
Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): New ASIC and GPU AI server racks in expansion

Wiwynn delivered strong Sep revenues, which were 19% ahead of our estimates, 
reflecting strong general server demand from US leading CSPs, and its major customers’ 
new ASIC AI server ramping up. We expect revenue YoY growth to remain strong at mid 
double-digit through Dec 2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +60 YoY / +8% QoQ, 
driven by (1) major customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up, across air-cooled and 
liquid-cooled, (2) general servers end demand remaining solid on agentic AI, (3) new 
GPU-powered AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26 across NVIDIA and 
AMD platforms, and (4) potential new customers in ASIC AI servers. As we highlighted in 
Oct (Global Server TAM update), we raised AI servers implied AI chips shipment by 9% / 
19% in 2027 / 28E with ASIC penetration rate increasing to 58% by 2028E, and our US 
internet team forecasts that leading US cloud service providers (CSPs) in aggregate will 
grow their capex budgets by 84%/ 52%/ 13% YoY in 2026-28E, with our US CSP capex 
estimates raised by 4% / 17% / 22% in 2026-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E, supporting 
our positive view on Wiwynn. Maintain Buy with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$4,000

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$4,000. Our target price is 
based on 20.3x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.  
Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): AI server racks to see strong ramp-up with customer expansion and 
specification upgrades

Wistron’s Sep revenue was up 87% YoY to NT$381bn, or 18% lower than our estimate, 
which we attribute to the impact of shipment timing volatility in higher-ASP AI products. 
We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI 
server racks ramp-up in 4Q26, which should drive up the ASP , along with strong AI 
spending and expansion of the company’s customer base. The new GPU AI server racks 
have various racks across GPU, CPU, and LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming 
quarters. Sep PC shipment was up 7% MoM but down 32% YoY to 1.5m units, reflecting 
the slow PC demand amid the consumption pull-in under the high memory cost. Despite 
PC shipments growth likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in since late 
2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, driving 2H26 
revenue up +41% HoH. Maintain Buy with an unchanged TP of NT$291 (12.6x 2027E P/E 
vs. 30.4% NI YoY in 2027-28E).

Pegatron’s Sep revenue was up 87% YoY / 143% MoM to NT$215bn, or 64% higher than 
our estimate. This reflects a strong ramp-up in AI servers in Sep. Sep PC shipments were 
up c.8% MoM but down 44% YoY to 0.46m, reflecting the weak PC demand under the 
high memory costs. Prior to September, the company suffered from model transition (i.e. 
declining demand for older models while new models were in a slow shipment ramp-up 
phase).

Earnings revision: We factor in Pegatron’s Sep revenue and raise our 2026-28E net 
income by 4% / 6% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues and lower opex ratio. We raise our 
2026-28E revenues by 16% / 28% / 27%, considering the AI server shipment ramp-up 
and higher ASP on better specification. Given the much lower GM of AI server racks 
compared to Pegatron’s other products, our changes in gross profits narrow to 9% / 12% 
/ 15% in 2026E-28E. The opex ratio for 2026-28E is cut by 0.1 ppt/ 0.2 ppt/ 0.1 ppt to 
reflect the improved operational efficiency.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

## Document 135: 261028_ms_parade-DG

Source PDF: all report pdf/261028_ms_parade-DG.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-28

### Page 1

M
Idea
3Q26 earnings missed: Please see Exhibit 1 .
4Q guidance reflects weak NB demand: Parade is guiding rev. to decline 24% Q/Q
at mid-point, 32% below consensus. We overestimated the stability of demand from
standard-plus customers, while demand from standard customers remains very
weak, as expected. Based on the current demand outlook into 1Q27, it is difficult to
see a V-shaped recovery. That said, we appreciate Parade's supply chain
management and its ability to pass cost pressures on to customers. We see Parade's

TTED business offering L T opportunities amid PCB price hikes.
New business ramp could take time: We previously modelled the company’s ASIC-
like business contributing 15-20% of revenue in 2027-28. Given the complexity of
the chip design and weak DT demand, we believe we were too optimistic. As for the
TTED business, the company expects shipments to exceed 5mn units in 2026, which
could account for ~8% of 2026 revenue. The company expects NB shipments to

remain similar in 2027 vs. 2026, subject to DRAM pricing. Currently, we do not
expect DRAM pricing to decline meaningfully.
Double downgrade to UW; PT NT$444: We cut our earnings forecasts mainly to
reflect weaker 4Q guidance and a lower contribution from the ASIC-like business.
We lowered 2026/27/28e earnings by 14%/39%/43%. The company is now trading at
17x 2027e EPS. We like Parade's long-term growth potential but would need to see a
meaningful resumption in business growth to turn more positive. (continued on the

next page)

3Q26 earnings review: 3Q revenue was NT$4.2bn, -2% Q/Q, -4% Y/Y, and 4% above MSe; 
3Q GM was 40.3%, flattish Q/Q, -2.2ppt Y/Y and 2.3ppt below MSe; 3Q EPS was NT$7.67, 
flat Q/Q, -27% Y/Y and 6% below MSe. Weaker 2H26 demand has been due to OEM pull-
ins in 1H, compressed consumer demand due to price hikes, and compressed enterprise 
budgets due to allocation to AI spending.
Exhibit 1: 3Q26 earnings review
Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research (E) estimates
Why it's not too late to downgrade now: The weak 4Q26 outlook lowers the earnings 
base, while visibility on a sustained recovery remains limited. With NB shipments expected 
to remain broadly flat in 2027, TTED growth and initial ASIC-like contributions may be 
insufficient to offset weakness in the core business. The risk of prolonged earnings 
pressure and a slower-than-expected ramp in new businesses supports our more cautious 
view beyond the near-term reset.

Parade: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target from  NT$818 to NT$444:  Our price target is our base case
scenario value, which we continue to derive from a residual income model. Our bull and
bear case scenario values are cut from NT$948 to NT$755, and from NT$418 to NT$288,
respectively.
Intermediate growth cut to 9.4% from 11.4% to reflect slower ramp up of new businesses.

• 9.8% cost of equity (beta 1.3, risk-free rate 2% and risk premium 6%)

• Terminal growth rate of 4.0%

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 147: 261029_daiwa_Win Semiconductors

Source PDF: all report pdf/261029_daiwa_Win Semiconductors.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following our results memo, we raise our forecasts and TP 
for Win Semiconductors (WinSemi), but lower our rating by one notch to 
Outperform (2) on valuation grounds. While its topline trajectory appears to 
be unfolding intact, its margin trajectory is running better than our 
expectations. This, together with management’s positive commentary 
across its key business lines of RF, infrastructure and optics, reinforces our 
thesis that 3 growth pillars will turn around WinSemi’s bottom line and 
optimise its structural profitability (Upgrading: profit optimisation round the 
corner, 24 July). We suggest accumulating only on pullbacks.

What’s the impact: Forecast revisions. We raise our 2027-28E EPS by 
2-11% for WinSemi to factor in its higher margin trajectory, primarily on 
better product mix geared towards the infrastructure business, followed by 
the ramp-up in optics for AI optical communications (OC). We note the 
surge in our 2026E EPS revision is a result of the company’s non-operating 
gains of TWD684m in 3Q26, mainly from its 23%-owned ITEQ (6213 TT, 
TWD778, Buy [1], covered by Sheng Cheng), which distorts WinSemi’s 
growth trajectory at the bottom line into 2027.

Transformation paying off. At its results call, management commented on 
resilient RF demand from iOS and for WiFi routers, strong demand for low 
Earth orbit (LEO) satellite communications, and a continued ramp in pin 
diode (PD) sales after the product entered mass-production from late-
2Q26. This should be followed by laser diode (LD), such as continuous 
wave (CW) and electro-absorption modulated laser (EML), though 
customer qualifications will take time. All the commentary reinforces our 
thesis that 3 growth pillars will help turn around WinSemi’s bottom line for 
profitability optimisation after its business transformation: 1) RF business 
transformation paying off with premiumisation across cellular and WiFi for 
differentiation, 2) high-margin infrastructure scaling up on a robust LEO 
build, and 3) AI OC ramping fast, with broadened offerings from peripheral 
chips (optical drivers and modulators) to core chips for PD and LD.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we lift our 12M TP for 
WinSemi to TWD620 (previous: TWD449), based on a higher 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 55x (previous: 50x), justified by its 2025-28E EPS 
CAGR of 54% (previous: 50%) from a PEG perspective. Yet we lower our 
rating by one notch to Outperform (2) on valuation grounds, with key 
downside risk of commodity cost hikes worsening consumer demand.

How we differ: Our 2026E EPS is 40% above consensus due to our 
inclusion of the non-operating gains in 3Q26, while our 2027E EPS is 9% 
above likely on our higher margin assumptions.

29 October 2026

Downgrading: profit optimisation on the go

➢ Raising our forecasts on a higher margin trajectory 
➢ Growth pillars paying off across infrastructure, optics and RF 
➢ Downgrading our rating to Outperform (2), with a higher TP of TWD620

Company profile 
Founded in 1999, Win Semiconductors Corp (WinSemi) is the world’s largest compound semiconductor 
foundry, focusing on gallium-arsenide (GaAs) foundry services for customers in both wireless and wire-
line communication markets, as well as infrastructure applications. It has a diverse technology portfolio 
of processes that supports microwave frequency requirements from 50MHz to 100GHz, with 
applications including smartphones, tablet PCs, cellular base-stations, very small aperture terminals 
(VSAT) and fibre optics. It has started penetrating into vertical cavity surface emitting laser (VCSEL) 
foundries since 2017 for consumer laser applications such as 3D laser sensors and cameras.

### Page 5

5

Win Semiconductors (3105 TT): 29 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 4 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
WinSemi's board of directors (BoD) has 10 members, including 3 independent directors,
representing 30% of total directors. Among the BoD, three of its non-independent directors are
also executive management.
Capital management 2
Since 2017, its dividend payout ratio has remained above 58%, except for 2024 when no
dividend was paid. Despite being the largest compound semiconductor foundry in the world,
WinSemi is continually expanding its capacity for future growth. Thus, we see the balance
between investment and dividend as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1 It did not have any meaningful related-party transactions in 2023.
S Supply chainmanagement 2
WinSemi We actively cooperate with local suppliers and improve their development capabilities,
reduce carbon emissions and create local employment opportunities. WinSemi also conducts on-
site audits of suppliers and annual assessment of suppliers on environmental protection and
safety & health management systems. In 2023, 100% of WinSemi's new suppliers signed the
"WIN Supplier Code of Conduct".'"
E Water & wastewatermanagement 1
Effluent that flows out of WinSemi’s facilities will first undergo pre-treatment in-house and then
treatment by the wastewater treatment plant at Hwaya Technology Park before discharge. The
plant has an acid and alkali wastewater recycling system and chemical mechanical polishing
wastewater recycling system for wastewater management.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
WinSemi has established a hazardous substance-free management procedure and also
collaborates with its suppliers to produce products that are hazardous substance-free for
customers and consumers. WinSemi specifies in purchase contracts and order forms with its
suppliers that the provided materials may not contain hazardous substances as defined by
WinSemi.
For waste management, it aims to increase the recycling rate of waste solvents and reduce the
use of raw materials in production.
E GHG emissions 1
For reduction of energy consumption, it prioritises high-efficiency and low energy consumption
models when replacing equipment and has also established annual energy conservation
management objectives. WinSemi targets to reduce electricity consumption (kWh/6"e wafer) by
12% from 2018 to 458.58 and PFCs emission (ton CO2e/6"e wafer) by 50% from 2018 to 0.114
in 2030.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Taiwan Semis: GUC (3443.TW): Mgmt. Call takeaways: Server CPU ramp 
to sustain momentum into 2027; opportunities across applications
31 October 2026 | 12:35AM HKT
We talked to GUC (3443.TW, not covered) management recently. The company’s Sep 
revenues were up 22% MoM (vs. +1% MoM in Aug), reflecting its revenues from CPU 
of a leading US CSP in strong ramp up with more foundry capacity rolling out, and 
would continue to support the growth in 4Q26 and 2027 per the company. The 
positive tone echoes our positive view on Global Servers TAM, which we expect 
leading US cloud service providers (CSPs) in aggregate will grow their capex budgets 
by 84%/ 52%/ 13% YoY in 2026-28E, with our estimates being raised by 4% / 17% / 
22% in 2026-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E. Revenues in 3Q26 are driven by both 
NRE and turnkey solutions, with turnkey solutions revenues up 44% QoQ on the CPU 
ramp up, and despite turnkey solutions up to ~90% of 3Q26 revenues, the GM 
sustained at 20%+ in 3Q26, thanks to higher-margin NRE milestones. GUC adjusts 
pricing to reflect rising wafer and memory costs, and management sees memory 
impact is manageable on its customers’ projects. On competition, management 
highlighted its position as a core partner of TSMC, with in-house IP for 3D stacking as 
a key differentiator. GUC’s strong turnkey pull-in from its CSP customer’s server CPU 
and its focus on IP for TSMC 3D stacking point to continued demand for TSMC’s 
(2330.TW, Buy) advanced nodes and packaging. The positive tone on ASIC demand 
also echoes our positive view on rising ASIC penetration rate (~60% by 2028E, report 
link), supported by leading CSPs diversifying thier AI chips platforms and reducing 
token per second per costs, driving inferencing / training efficiency.

Company profile: GUC (3443.TW, not covered) is a ASIC design service company 
and a TSMC affiliate, providing NRE design services and turnkey production for

### Page 2

custom chips manufactured at TSMC. Its customers span cloud (CPUs, AI accelerators,
BMCs), automotive and consumer (image sensors, SSD controllers), with 68% of its
products under 7nm.
Key takeaways
1. Mass production as the key profit driver: Management noted that the key to
profitability lies in converting NRE projects into mass production. With the largest
customer’s CPU now in volume production, turnkey has become the main growth driver.
NRE carries higher GM than turnkey, but as it is recognized by milestone, quarterly NRE

margin varies with the mix of projects at different stages.
2. Expanding opportunities across cloud and automotive: GUC continues to open up
new opportunities across applications. In cloud, it works with all major US CSPs on
projects spanning CPUs and GPUs and also supplies other server projects such as BMCs,
while actively pursuing further AI accelerator opportunities. In automotive, it is engaged
with both US and Chinese customers at the NRE stage, two of which already plan for

mass production.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

## Document 213: 90天暴增近1万亿！AI表外负债悄然突破4.1万亿美元

Source PDF: all report pdf/90天暴增近1万亿！AI表外负债悄然突破4.1万亿美元.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-01
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

AI 基建热潮正催⽣巨额表外负担，七家科技公司相关承诺已达 4.11 万亿美元，约 90 天激增
近 1 万亿美元，远超表内债务。数据中⼼租约、采购义务与担保持续扩张，评级机构⼝径尚
不统⼀，市场开始担忧：若 AI 收⼊增⻓赶不上承诺兑现，巨额⽀出或将反噬企业现⾦流与
估值。
摘要：
AI 基础设施建设狂潮之下，⼀场规模空前的表外负债⻛暴正在硅⾕悄然积聚。
据最新季报数据，以 Alphabet 、 Meta 、微软、亚⻢逊为核⼼的超⼤规模云计算企业及 Nvidia 、
Broadcom 、 SpaceX ，其披露的表外承诺总规模已达 4.11 万亿美元，较摩根⼠丹利 8 ⽉末统计的
3.12 万亿美元增加近 9920 亿美元 —— 约 90 天内新增承诺接近 1 万亿美元。如果把摩根⼠丹利此前
统计中纳⼊的、基于新闻报道⽽⾮正式财报披露的表外承诺也计算在内，总额将达到 4.29 万亿美
元。
与此同时，上述公司账⾯上的实际债务和租赁负债合计约为 1 万亿美元，意味着每 1 美元的表内
负债背后，对应着超过 4 美元未在资产负债表上显⽰的承诺。

这⼀数字仍未触顶。 Nvidia 将于 11 ⽉下旬发布季报， Oracle 和 Broadcom 则于 12 ⽉披露，三家公
司均尚未更新⾄最新季度数据。⽬前的 4.1 万亿美元，只是下限。⾼盛⾸席信贷策略师 Amanda
Lynam 亦指出，投资者对上述表外敞⼝的关注度持续升温，并警⽰各⼤评级机构在处理⽅式上
尚⽆统⼀⼝径 —— 标普对表外租赁进⾏杠杆调整，⽽穆迪和惠誉则未做相应处理，评级⽅法论
的潜在变动值得持续关注。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

单季新增 577 亿美元，增速毫⽆放缓迹象
为精确呈现第三季度的变化，可将已提交三季报的五家公司与其⼆季度数据进⾏对⽐。这五家
公司的合并表外承诺从 2.48 万亿美元增⾄ 3.05 万亿美元，单季增幅 5770 亿美元，增幅 23% 。
各公司具体情况如下：
Meta 新增 1950 亿美元，增幅 29% 。不可撤销的采购承诺从 3490 亿美元飙升⾄ 4550 亿美元，主
要涉及 2027 ⾄ 2028 年及以后的第三⽅云计算容量（交易对⼿未予披露）。尚未启⽤的已签署租
约从 2790 亿美元增⾄ 3560 亿美元，其中包括 7 ⽉签署的 680 亿美元租约；其在路易斯安那州
Hyperion 数据中⼼及德克萨斯州埃尔帕索数据中⼼合资项⽬上的剩余价值担保合计达 415 亿美
元。值得注意的是，拥有 970 亿美元经营现⾦流、信⽤评级为 Aa3/AA- 的 Meta ，在财报发布后信
⽤违约掉期（ CDS ）触及历史⾼位，随即宣布最⾼ 950 亿美元的股权融资计划，其中 375 亿美元

已完成，另有约 600 亿美元的 ATM 项⽬待执⾏。

### Page 3

亚⻢逊新增 1310 亿美元，增幅 41% ，为超⼤规模云企业中百分⽐涨幅最⼤。⽆条件采购义务⼏
乎翻倍，从 1300 亿美元增⾄ 2540 亿美元，新增⼀项 1596 亿美元的 " 物业及设备 " 条⽬；尚未启⽤
租约从 1370 亿美元增⾄ 1650 亿美元。唯⼀抵消项是向 OpenAI 尚⽋的 213 亿美元已于三季度⼊
账，转移⾄资产负债表。受资本⽀出超预期冲击，亚⻢逊股价上周五⼤跌。
Alphabet 新增 1640 亿美元，增幅 18% 。采购承诺上升 950 亿美元，尚未启⽤租约从 850 亿增⾄
1310 亿美元，能源设备担保（期限延伸⾄ 2049 年）从 76 亿美元翻倍⾄ 162 亿美元，并新增⼀项
66 亿美元的 TPU 硬件回购担保。 Alphabet 在此前已向股权市场融资 800 亿美元、⾃由现⾦流持续
告负的背景下，其表外承诺已突破 1 万亿美元⼤关，成为⾸家单⼀公司表外承诺超过 1 万亿美元
的企业，达到 10590 亿美元。
微软新增 350 亿美元，增幅 6% ，是其中表外承诺扩张相对 " 克制 " 的⼀家。三季度新签数据中⼼租

约约 530 亿美元，其中 170 亿美元已启⽤并转⼊资产负债表，尚未启⽤租约从 3290 亿美元增⾄
3650 亿美元。需注意的是，微软的采购和建设承诺每年仅在 10-K 中披露⼀次，⽬前的 2290 亿美
元数据仍停留在 6 ⽉末，实际数字届时⼤概率将⼤幅上修。即便如此， " 最⼲净 " 的超⼤规模云企
业近期仍需发⾏ 250 亿美元债券融资。
SpaceX 新增 510 亿美元，增幅 174% ，增速最为激进。其表外承诺从⼆季度末的 290 亿美元增⾄
三季度末的 400 亿美元，再到 10 ⽉ 26 ⽇通过 8-K ⽂件披露的 400 亿美元 Apollo GPU 主融资租赁协
议（租赁期定于 2027 年上半年开始执⾏）后，总规模跃升⾄ 800 亿美元。
评级机构⼝径不⼀，市场开始⾃⾏定价
⾼盛的 Amanda Lynam 指出，若将五家主要超⼤规模云企业的表外租约与采购承诺合并统计，
相关规模⾃ 2023 年以来已增⻓⼋倍。然⽽，这⼀⻛险敞⼝在评级体系中并未获得统⼀对待：标
普在计算杠杆时对表外租赁进⾏调整，⽽穆迪与惠誉则未做同等处理，评级⽅法论的潜在变化

因此成为值得密切追踪的⻛险点。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
这种会计处理上的不透明，已开始引发市场的直接反应。分析机构 Needham 指出，⼀旦将表外
负债纳⼊计算， Meta 的企业价值将提升 35% 。 Meta 在财报披露后 CDS 创下历史新⾼，随即宣布
⼤规模股权融资。 Lynam 亦强调，部分企业已明确表⽰需要在市场趋于饱和之前提前锁定资
本，这⼀表述被认为是 Meta 股权融资的直接注脚，且未必是最后⼀例。
表外承诺扩张的三⼤构成
从结构上拆解这 4.1 万亿美元，可⻅三条主线：
⼀是尚未启⽤的已签署租约，总规模已达 1.39 万亿美元，较 8 ⽉时的 1.12 万亿美元进⼀步扩⼤。
这些资产通常由特殊⽬的载体（ SPV ）出资建设的数据中⼼外壳构成，只有在正式通电启⽤
后，才会转⼊公司资产负债表。
⼆是采购承诺，规模为 2.25 万亿美元，较 8 ⽉末的 1.73 万亿美元⼤幅攀升。这⼀类别包含了 Meta

斥巨资锁定的第三⽅云计算容量、亚⻢逊新增的物业设备采购义务等。
三是担保与兜底承诺，已从 1580 亿美元跃升⾄ 2730 亿美元，包括 Alphabet 的能源设备担保及
TPU 硬件回购担保，以及 Meta 在两个数据中⼼合资项⽬上的剩余价值担保。
表外债务扩张，市值同步蒸发
摩根⼠丹利曾于 8 ⽉警告，这些承诺在算⼒供应受约束期间具有 " 战略价值 " ，但 " 若供需在预期之
前趋于正常化，企业将不得不为过剩产能买单，或试图重新谈判合同条款 " 。
市场已开始给出答案。 Alphabet 股价本⽉经历创纪录的单⽇暴跌，亚⻢逊持续⾛低，纳斯达克
100 指数成份股本⽉合计市值蒸发逾 1.2 万亿美元。
换⾔之，⼀个季度内，⼤型科技公司新增约 1 万亿美元表外负债；⼀个⽉内，这些公司的市值缩
⽔同样超过 1 万亿美元。表外承诺不因股价涨跌⽽消失，到期必须兑付。当前的核⼼悬念在于：
AI 商业化收⼊能否在下⼀轮承诺浪潮到来之前实现追赶 —— ⽽下⼀个 10-Q 季报季，距今并不遥
远。

### Page 5

## Document 245: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기 환율 부진에도 영업이익 100조는 넘긴다 
삼성전자 26년 3분기 실적은 이전 전망치를 하회할 전망이다. 매출액은 195.2조원으
로 이전 분기 대비 13.8% 증가하고, 영업이익은 101.7조원으로 13.6% 증가할 것으
로 예상한다. 영업이익률은 52.1%로 전 분기 대비 0.1%p 하락할 전망이다. 원/달러 
환율 영향이 주요 변수로 작용한 것으로 추정한다. 2분기에 집행되지 않았던 성과급 
일부가 3분기에 반영된 영향도 있을 것으로 예상한다. DS 부문은 매출액 149.9조원, 
영업이익은 101.8조원으로 추정하고, 디스플레이 매출액 8.8조원, 영업이익 0.9조
원, MX/네트워크는 매출액 34.3조원, 영업적자 1.1조원으로 예상한다. VD/가전도 
영업적자 규모가 커질 것으로 예상한다.  
주주환원은 주가 상승을 견인할 것 
8월 21일 이후 주주환원에 대한 관심이 커졌다. 자사주 규모는 크지 않겠지만 배당
을 통한 주주환원 규모는 사상 최대 규모일 것으로 예상한다. 글로벌 대표 IT업체와 
비교해도 손색없는 수준이다. 메모리 업체의 주주환원 규모가 100조원 넘게 집행된 
적이 없어서 주가에 어떠한 영향을 미칠 지가 관전 포인트인데 긍정적 역할을 할 것
으로 기대한다. 자사주를 통한 EPS 개선은 아니지만, 안정적이고 지속적인 규모의 
주주환원은 주가를 한층 더 높은 수준에서 평가 받는 계기가 될 것으로 판단한다.  
현저한 저평가 구간, 이전 전망 유지 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 메모리 공급 부족 상황이 장기화될 
것으로 전망하고, 이로 인해 수익성은 현재 수준에서 유지되고, 이익 규모는 안정적
으로 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 현저히 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 목
표주가는 460,000원을 유지한다.

### Page 4

삼성전자 (005930)

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
반도체 Check Point 
1. 차별화된 주주환원 전략. 배경은 안정적 이익 규모 
2026년에는 대대적인 규모의 주주환원이 예상된다. 100 조원 내외 수준으로 
예상하는데 상당 부분은 배당으로 집행될 것으로 추정한다. 배당성향이 25%가 유지될 
것으로 예상하기 때문이다. 자사주 매입 여력은 20조원 내외 수준일 것으로 예상한다. 
주주환원은 주가 밸류 상승에 기여할 것으로 기대한다. 일회성이 아니고 장기적으로 
높은 수준의 주주환원이 가능하다는 것은 싸이클 변동에 따른 이익의 변동성이 
낮다는 의미이기도 하다. 
2. AI 모델 개발 속도 조절. 그래도 메모리 수요를 줄일 수는 없다 
Anthropic CEO는 현재 AI 개발 속도가 지나치게 빨라서 인간의 능력을 뛰어 넘는 
임계점에 이를 가능성이 높아져서 개발 속도를 늦출 필요가 있다고 언급했다. 이를 
Meta, Google, SpaceX 수장들이 공감했다. 우선 이들의 공감으로 AI 모델의 개발 
속도가 둔화될 것으로 보고 있지 않다. 또한, 이를 가정하더라도 향후 AI 모델은 
Agent AI로 진화할 것으로 보고 있다. 이에 따른 메모리에 대한 필요성은 급격히 
증가할 것으로 기대한다. 
3. 자신감 넘치는 HBM  
HBM4가 2026년 하반기부터 주력 제품이 될 것으로 전망한다. 26 년 3분기에는 
비중이 40% 내외, 4 분기에는 60%를 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 연초 기대 대비로는 
낮은 수준이다. 삼성전자의 점유율만 보면 연초 대비 높은 수준의 점유율이 예상된다.  
HBM4 물량이 예상보다 늦게 본격화되면서 2026년 HBM 시장 규모는 이전 전망 
대비 축소될 전망이다. HBM4도 늦은 출시로 연내 생산 물량은 이전 전망치인 130억 
Gb을 하회할 것으로 예상한다.  
2027년 HBM4 가격이 최근에 확정이 되었고, 이를 통해서 DRAM 수준의 수익성 
확보가 가능할 것으로 기대한다. 수익성 발목을 잡았던 HBM은 2027년 물량부터는 
영업이익 개선의 변수가 될 것으로 전망한다.

장기적 주주환원은 
안정적 이익이 뒷받침 
Agent AI는 메모리 
수요를 증가시킨다 
HBM4 안정적 진입한 
것으로 판단

동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일

## Document 255: [034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡_62bfa2bd

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡_62bfa2bd.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

Company Brief 
유틸리티/건설 
Analyst 문경원, CFA 
kyeongwon.moon@meritz.co.kr 
RA 이동석 
dongseok.lee@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 Review: 바닥을 잡는다면 지금  
믹스 개선에 따른 이익률 개선 조금씩 관찰 중 
3Q26 연결 영업이익은 2,612억원(+90.6% YoY) 을 기록하며 컨센서스(2,455억원)
를 소폭 상회했다. 밥캣(1,674억원)이 컨센서스(1,761억원)를 소폭 하회한 가운데, 
에너빌리티 부문 영업이익(+179% YoY, OPM 6.6%) 이 호실적을 이끌었다. 환율 하
락에도 불구, 가스터빈, 원전 등 고수익성 프로젝트의 비중 상승이 주요 원인이다. 
3Q26 누적 수주는 10.0조원(+85.5% YoY) 을 기록했다. 4Q26 가스복합 EPC 수주 
등을 감안하면 연간 가이던스(13.3조원) 달성은 무난하다. 한수원과의 협상 결렬로 
이목이 집중된 체코 두코바니 시공 수주는 2027년 인식될 확률이 높아 보인다. 주간
사가 바뀌더라도 이미 프로젝트에 참여 중인 동사를 배제하기는 쉽지 않다.

대미투자 이후, 연말~연초 수주 모멘텀에 집중 
연말 연초에 다음과 수주 모멘텀이 집중되면서, 주가 상승을 이끌 전망이다. 
1) 대형원전: 정부 자금 중심의 Westinghouse LLI(장납기 기자재) 발주가 몰린다. 
우선 미국 DOE(에너지부)가 제공한 대출을 기반으로 미국 수주가 기대된다.  폴란드 
정부 역시 최근 LLI 발주 작업을 시작했다. 한편, 한국 정부는 연말까지 100억 달러 
규모의 대미 투자 송금을 협의 중인데, 이 역시 DOC(상무부) 주도의 대형 원전 건설
을 위한 LLI 발주에 쓰일 가능성이 높다 
2) SMR: 오래 기다려왔던 Nuscale 향 수주가 시작되고 있다. 우선 29일 모듈 1기 
제작 계약을 체결했다. 현금이 부족한 Nuscale이 용기를 냈다는 건, 곧 Nuscale-
TVA 간 PPA 가 임박했음을 의미한다. 본격적인 수주는 PPA 직후 예상된다.  
3) 가스 터빈: 2026년 초 해외 수주(7기)는 2029년까지 인도 계획이다. 이후 2030
년 슬랏 예약을 완료하고 2031년 물량을 논의 중으로 보인다. 지금은 2030년 슬랏
에 대한 PO가 나와 수주잔고에 인식할 타이밍이다. 역시 수출 물량으로 추측한다.

성장에 대한 기대가 사라진 밸류에이션, 지금이 바닥 
투자의견 Buy를 유지, 할인율 상승을 반영하여 적정주가를 109,000원으로 -17% 
하향 조정한다. 금리 상승, 환율 하락, 트럼프 지지율 하락 등 Top-down 리스크에 
최근 조정을 받았지만, 추가적인 주가 하락 폭은 제한적일 전망이다. EV/선행 수주잔
고 지표는 현재 1.0배 수준까지 하락하며, 원전의 성장성이 주목받지 못했던 
2021~2024년 수준에 근접하게 내려왔다.  
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 30

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 109,000원 
현재주가 (10.29) 56,700원 
상승여력 92.2%

### Page 1

2026.10.29   
HD현대중공업  (329180)

3Q26: 조선사는 우직해야 한다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 5조 7,536억원/영업이익 8,992억원 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 매출은 5조 7,536억원/영업이익은 8,992억원으로 
컨센서스(6조 1,295억원/9,671억원) 및 당사 추정치(6조 859억원/9,679억원) 대비 
매출 및 영업이익 각각 -6.1%/-7.0%, -5.5%/-7.1%를 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다.  
조선사업부 매출이 4조 3,988억원/영업이익 6,463억원(OPM 14.7%)이며 이중 상선 
OPM은 14.7%로, 2분기 17.8%에 비해 3.1%P 하락하였으나 1분기 15.9%와 크게 
차이나지 않는 수준을 유지하면서 조업일수 및 환율 하락에도 불구하고 P-MIX및 
빈티지 개선에 따른 효과를 보여주었다. 해양사업부는 매출 3,799억원/영업이익 
1,265억원(OPM 33.3%)로 지난 분기(15.9%)보다도 훨씬 높은 영업이익을 
기록하였다. 엔진기계사업부는 매출 9,511억원/영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)를 
기록하며 역시 견조한 실적을 기록하였다.  
일회성 이익으로 Trion 공사의 C/O 180 억원, 일회성 손실로는 셧다운에 따른 
조업감소 영향 500억원 및 환율하락에 따른 이익 감소분 450억원이 있었으며 금액은 
공개되지 않았으나 임단협 잠정타결에 따른 성과급/타결금 지급에 대한 추가 충당금의 
75% 인식이 반영되었다. 알려진 일회성만 제거하더라도 실질 영업이익률은 
17%이상으로 2분기 16.4%보다 높아진 호실적이라고 할 수 있다. 특히 RUYA 공사가 
본 궤도에 오르면서 해양사업부의 이익률이 괄목할 만큼 높아졌으며 지속가능하다는 
회사의 설명이다.

투자의견 BUY 및 조선업종 TOP PICK 유지, 목표주가 530,000원 하향 
HD현대중공업의 목표주가를 530,000원으로 38.3% 하향하지만, 투자의견 BUY와 
조선업종 TOP PICK 을 유지한다. 환율 하락 및 28년 하반기 엔진증설 매출확대, 
해양공사 이익률 상승 등 요인을 종합 반영하여 실적 추정치를 미세 반영하여 도출한 
2027F BPS 131,910원에 Target P/B를 기존대비 하향한 4.0배로 적용하였다. 특수선 
기대감 및 선가상승 기대감 하락에 따른 조선업종 전반의 밸류에이션 하락을 반영하여 
Target을 낮췄지만 그럼에도 조선업종 중 가장 높은 멀티플을 부여하며 Top 
Pick의견을 견지한다. 여러 악재에도 불구하고 HD현대중공업은 탄탄하다. 본업인 
상선사업부의 9월말 기준 수주량은 137.9억 달러로 목표치를 이미 초과 달성하였으며 
전사 기준으로도 수주목표 90.5%를 채워넣었다. 수주잔고 또한 상선 421억 
달러/전사 609억 달러로 각각 작년 말 대비 12.6%/13.2% 증가하였다. 해양부문과 
엔진기계부문은 조선사업부보다도 높은 이익률로 든든한 실적의 기반이 되고 있다.  
최근 HD현대그룹은 미국의 EPC강자 Kiewit과 미국 합작법인 설립에 관한 계약을 
맺고 그린필드 기반의 미국 신규 조선소 건설을 추진하고 있다. 빠르면 올해 중으로 
건립과 관련한 세부 사항이 공개될 예정이며 HD현대중공업이 중추적인 역할을 담당할 
것으로 보인다. 일찍부터 브라운필드 방식으로 접근한 동종사와 달리 다소 시간은 
걸렸지만 과감하게 결정된 투자다. 조선소 예상 준공 역시 2028년 이후로 예상되어 
당장 기업가치에 반영하기는 어려우나 엔진 증설과 함께 2028년 후에는 분명한 실적 
상향 요인이 될 것으로 예상한다. 우직하게 본업에 충실하며 신중하게 미래를 준비하는 
조선소, 귀감이다.

당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상

 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

## Document 288: AI最危险的交易或许不在科技股，而在美元

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI最危险的交易或许不在科技股，而在美元.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

德意志银⾏警告， AI 泡沫若破裂，冲击可能从美股蔓延⾄美元。外国投资者持有逾 20 万亿
美元美国股票，若股市、美元与美国实际利率同步下⾏，传统汇率对冲机制或将失效， AI
抛售潮可能演变为美元危机。
摘要：
当 AI 泡沫开始消退，最⼤的⻛险可能不是纳斯达克回调，⽽是美元崩塌。外国投资者持有逾 20
万亿美元的美国股票资产，⼀旦 AI 驱动的抛售引发股市与美元同步下跌，市场将⾯临远超技术性
修正的宏观冲击。
德意志银⾏警告， AI 抛售潮可能触发美国股市、货币对冲与美元之间的危险反馈循环。与以往科
技股回调不同，这⼀次美元的传统避险属性正受到根本质疑 —— 强美元未必能再充当外国投资
者的损失缓冲垫。
如果纳斯达克、美元指数（ DXY ）与美国实际利率三者同步下⾏，市场正在定价的将不仅是仓
位的技术性调整，⽽是对整个 AI 繁荣周期的系统性重估。这意味着下⼀轮 AI 抛售，有可能演变为
⼀场美元危机。
20 万亿美元的脆弱敞⼝
据德意志银⾏估算，外国投资者持有的美国股票资产中，约 90% 的价值来⾃累积的价格升值，

⽽⾮实际净购⼊。换⾔之，这⼀庞⼤头⼨⾼度依赖美国股市估值的持续维持。
更关键的是，外国股票资⾦流⼊⽬前已承担了美国经常账⼾⾚字的全部融资需求，美元因此与
股票市场周期深度绑定。⼀旦股市出现⼤幅调整，美元的资⾦来源将直接承压。
对冲机制在美国本⼟冲击下失效
过去⼗年，每当美国股市下跌，美元⾛强往往帮助外国投资者对冲了部分损失。但这⼀保护机
制有⼀个前提：冲击来⾃外部。若⻛险源头在美国本⾝，股市与美元可能同向下跌，对冲逻辑
随之⽡解。
StoneX 的数据显⽰， AI 情绪与美元之间的五⽇滚动相关系数⾼达 +0.97 ，⽽ 20 ⽇相关系数仅为
+0.31 。这意味着短期内两者已形成⾼度同步 ——AI 信⼼⼀旦动摇，美元将同步承压。
对于未对冲的外国投资者⽽⾔，股票和汇率的双重亏损将造成双重打击。即便他们不抛售股
票，单纯提⾼货币对冲⽐例的操作本⾝，也会形成额外的抛售美元压⼒，加剧汇率下⾏。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

实际利率是关键验证信号
德意志银⾏认为， AI 资本开⽀预期的急速膨胀，是推动美国股市与实际利率同步⾛⾼的重要驱动
⼒之⼀。因此，实际利率的⾛向将成为判断市场性质的关键信号。
若科技股、美元与美国实际利率三者同步下⾏，将表明投资者开始折现⼀个更疲弱的 AI 投资周
期 —— 这将把⼀次技术性股市修正，升级为更⼴泛的宏观重新定价。相反，若实际利率维持⾼
位，则调整更可能只是仓位层⾯的波动。
做空美元或成 AI 修正的隐性交易
⾯对 AI 潜在回调，最直观的交易是做空科技股。但德意志银⾏指出，不那么显眼、却可能更具威
⼒的交易是做空美元。
⼀旦以美国为中⼼的股票冲击成真，外国投资者将被迫重新审视其在这 20 万亿美元资产上的货
币对冲策略，传统的股票 - 美元正相关关系可能发⽣逆转。投资者需要同时盯紧纳斯达克、 DXY
与美国实际利率三个指标。若三者共同⾛低，市场所定价的⻛险将远⼤于⼀次普通的仓位出清
—— 下⼀轮 AI 抛售，或将以⼀场美元抛售收场。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The U.S. Capex Boom No One is Talking About—And Its Implications
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
We have long been of the belief that the U.S. is on the cusp of an inbound FDI capex boom
that can result in numerous positive spillover effects for the U.S. economy. The latest

figures from the U.S. Department of Commerce bear this out.
Exhibit 1 highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be inside the U.S.,
with FDI totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year. That is a staggering
103% jump from a year ago and places FDI inflows on track for a record year.
The latest inbound capex already adds to the massive scale of foreign investment in the
U.S. To wit, the accumulated stock of FDI in the U.S. stands at nearly $6T, based on the

latest figures from the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA). Manufacturing accounts for
roughly 43% of the total, and, at last count, some 10,000 foreign affiliates were operating
in the U.S.
These firms are largely invisible to the average U.S. investor, but the activities of U.S.
affiliates of foreign multinationals are substantial and competitive- and growth-enhancing.
To this point and based on the latest data from the BEA, foreign affiliates in the U.S.

employed nearly 8.6 million U.S. workers in 2024. That represents 6.1% of total private
sector employment, with manufacturing and retail the two most prominent sectors
of employment. Roughly 24% of all manufacturing jobs in the U.S. are from foreign
subsidiaries. In terms of pay, U.S. workers at these firms make $89,000 annually, 7% higher
than the national average, according to the Global Business Alliance.
Foreign affiliates also contributed some $1.5T to U.S. GDP; comprised some 15.7% of total

U.S. private sector investment; accounted for 12.4% of total U.S. research & development
(R&D) expenditures; paid roughly 20% of all U.S. federal taxes; and made up 22.5% of
U.S. exports and 26.2% of U.S. imports. 1 These are the direct effects of U.S. subsidiaries
of foreign companies operating in the U.S. The indirect effects are even greater in terms
of more income, jobs, growth and tax revenue—all bolstering the long-term competitive
advantage of the U.S. In a nutshell, foreign firms operating in the U.S. are an important

ingredient of U.S. economic growth.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Companies that once served the U.S. market exclusively through exports
increasingly face higher costs and more uncertainties and hence want to be “inside.”
Two, with a $32T-plus economy with extraordinary consumer purchasing power, foreign

firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment than just trade (exports). There
is no more dynamic consumer in the world than the American shopper.
Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers, semiconductors, power
generation, etc., has created numerous investment opportunities for many foreign firms
that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging capex cycle.
Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly developed

technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.
As more U.S. affiliates of foreign multinationals set up shop in the U.S., think more job
creation across the country, more manufacturing output, more locally sourced purchases,
more U.S. exports, more spending on R&D, and more tax revenue for local communities.
And over the long run, think a more globally competitive U.S. economy.
1 Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign Multinational Enterprises, 2024” and “Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign

Multinational Enterprises, 2023”, BEA July 2026.
Investment Implications
The boom in foreign investment in
the U.S. should continue to support
employment, productivity, corporate
earnings, and broader U.S. economic
competitiveness, in our view. Hence
our U.S. bias.
Exhibit 1: FDI Inflows to the U.S.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
And speaking of global competitiveness, Exhibit 2A is very illustrative of the magnet pull of
the U.S. economy to attract FDI versus the rest of the world. Of new greenfield investment
this decade, note the overriding preference for the U.S., accounting for nearly 16% of total
global greenfield investment this decade. America’s share is larger than the next three
countries combined—the United Kingdom, India, and Germany. And note that America’s

share of new investment is more than 5X that of China’s. Most of this new investment
has gone into manufacturing sectors like electrical equipment, computers, appliances and
batteries and has originated from Asia and Europe.
The bulk of foreign investment comes from Europe, followed by Japan and Canada, but as
Exhibit 2B highlights, there is a new dynamic playing out when it comes to America’s ability
to attract capital. There is a new source in town: a handful of developing nations (namely

Taiwan, South Korea, Mexico and Brazil). Mexican manufacturers, Taiwan technology
leaders, South Korea chip leaders, Brazil industrial firms, India pharmaceutical firms—these
companies and more are realizing that in today’s trade environment, the best way to serve
the American market is not necessarily through exports but through investment—investing
locally. FDI flows from developing nations reached $63B in the 12 months through June,
more than double the level a year prior ($24.5B), according to the BEA. Fueled by insatiable

AI demand, flows from South Korea alone totaled $23.4B in the first half of 2026. The
upshot is that an increasingly important source of capital for the U.S. is coming from an
unlikely source: the developing nations.
More fire for the capex boom. The coming boom in FDI inflows is supportive of our thesis
that the U.S. stands on the cusp of a major industrial upgrade. We are in the early innings
of a structural—not cyclical—capex cycle that should lead to the rebuilding of domestic

supply chains, higher value-added manufacturing, and more AI-driven productivity growth.
Foreign firms, in our opinion, will be part of this process.
That is important to recognize because remember the following: when a foreign company
builds a factory or an R&D facility or expands its existing manufacturing capacity right
here in the U.S., good things happen. Demand for U.S. goods and services goes up (as does
demand for construction, materials, logistics and professional services). New jobs are created,

boosting incomes of U.S. workers, while supporting more local spending and consumption.
And because many foreign firms often bring advanced manufacturing technologies when
investing in the U.S., the end results include a boost in productivity and innovation in various
sectors of the economy.
All the above, of course, are the secret ingredients to stronger U.S. economic growth, rising
corporate earnings and higher equity prices. With a little help from our friends, America’s

industrial renewal is gaining strength and momentum.

4 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Europe’s Electoral Cycle and Market Risks
Ehiwario Efeyini, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
As midterm elections approach for the U.S. next month, voters across Europe will also
enter their own election season starting this November and continuing throughout
2027. Over the next year, 52% of the total EU population, representing 46% of EU GDP
across eight countries, will go to the polls, including voters in three of its four largest

economies (Exhibit 3). Having announced snap general elections earlier this month,
Spain will vote for members of its lower and upper house on November 29. A series of
parliamentary elections are then due for 2027 in Estonia (by March 7), Finland (on April
18), Greece (by July 25), Slovakia (by September 28), Poland (by November 11) and Italy
(by December 22). And perhaps most consequentially France will stage Presidential
elections on April 18, with a potential second round of voting set for May 2.

For the EU as a whole, the significance of these coming elections will be the potential
for a shift toward more populist, domestically oriented policy priorities from leading
opposition candidates (particularly in France, Spain and Poland) which could obstruct the
process of closer EU integration. In the nearer term, adoption of the new seven-year
(2028-2034) EU budget due by the end of 2027 could be impeded. And over the longer
term, renewed efforts toward an EU-wide capital market union to deepen and attract

more inward investment into European markets could also be further delayed.
At the country level, investors have seen bond yields rise globally in 2026 on the back of
higher inflation and concerns over fiscal sustainability. The debt question has arguably
been most pressing in parts of Europe, especially given the likelihood that upcoming
elections make for domestic increases in government largesse. And of the eight
countries going to the polls over the next year, France remains the biggest source of

concern. Interest rate spreads on French 10-year sovereign bonds have overtaken those
in both Greece and Italy this year (Exhibit 4), driven by investor concerns over public
debt. The 2027 government budget unveiled earlier this month projected a deficit of
5.0% of GDP (well in excess of the 3% specified in the EU Stability and Growth Pact),
but remained insufficiently expansive to nullify the risk that the current government
could be unseated later this year if it is forced through the fragmented parliament. This

comes on top of an existing debt load of close to 120% of GDP and a halving of official
2026 growth forecasts from 1.0% at the start of the year to 0.5% in September. In
short, all four variables in France’s debt sustainability equation currently stand as major
headwinds, and the two leading candidates in the 2027 election have shown limited
appetite for fiscal consolidation over the coming years.
Exhibit 3: Europe's Upcoming Elections Represent Around Half of EU GDP and Population.

Investment Implications
We remain cautious on European
markets overall, despite seeing
pockets of strength in selected
areas such as defense, data center
infrastructure and renewable energy.
On top of growing uncertainty
from the upcoming electoral cycle

Europe under-exposed to the key
growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and

Intervention from the ECB under its 2022 bond purchase program is also unlikely at this

stage given that the spread widening in France has not appeared either disorderly or
unwarranted. Meanwhile, conditions could potentially be aggravated by expected ECB rate
hikes in response to still above-target inflation across the eurozone. Indeed recent moves
to tighten monetary policy from a number of major central banks have been read most
hawkishly in Europe. Most of the increase in European bond yields has come from higher
inflation breakevens as opposed to real yields in the U.S. and Japan. And despite some

paring back of tightening expectations in the wake of recent bond volatility, the market is
still pricing three more policy rate increases over the next 12 months.
Euro depreciation and an emerging shift toward more protectionist trade policy with China
could further complicate the ECB policy outlook. China posted a record $35B trade surplus,
according to Eurostat, with the EU in September (alongside its $938B total surplus for the
first three quarters of 2026), and the two markets have agreed to reduce the EU bilateral

deficit by capping imports of hybrid cars for four years. Hybrids account for less than 2%
of total EU imports from China, but this could potentially mark the start of a broader shift
toward more trade protection in Europe with its largest foreign supplier which risks adding
further upward pressure to cost-push inflation. And though the leading candidates in the
French election may favor resuming oil and gas imports from Russia, Europe also remains

among the most at risk from the global energy shock. Indeed some 56% of the region’s
crude oil consumption is in the form of diesel product according to the International Energy
Agency, more than twice the U.S. share.
The 2- to 10-year eurozone yield curve has already flattened significantly since the start
of the year. And at just over 30 basis points as of mid-October, it remains flatter than
its equivalents in the U.S., UK and Japan. Outside Energy and Technology, Financials have

been among the strongest-performing sectors in Europe this year. But additional ECB
rate hikes could lead to further curve flattening, compressing bank net interest margins
and contributing to slower growth—particularly given that rate increases are coming in
response to supply-driven inflation rather than reflecting stronger economic activity.
We nonetheless continue to see pockets of strength in European markets. Growing
defense budgets should remain a tailwind for Industrials as national governments look to

meet their North Atlantic Treaty Organization targets over the next several years. And the
sector should be further supported by indirect exposure to AI investment through electric
cabling, power infrastructure and building materials for data centers. Leaders in renewable
energy equipment and services are also likely to be beneficiaries of grid upgrades and
broadening electrification globally, particularly given elevated fossil fuel prices. But our view

on the region remains cautious overall. On top of growing uncertainty from the upcoming
electoral cycle and interest rate outlook, rising competition and slowing demand from
China continue to challenge key segments of Europe’s Consumer Discretionary sector.
Limited direct AI concentration in technology hardware, cloud services and application
software also leaves Europe underexposed to the key growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and emerging Asia.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
Cautious Optimism Entering Fiscal Year 2027
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
“A renewed focus on rebuilding fiscal space is urgent, given elevated debt, higher
borrowing costs, multiplying demands on public budgets and heightened uncertainty.” So
warned the IMF about the global fiscal outlook last week in its updated World Economic

Outlook report. Much is at stake for developed nations in Europe and Asia facing high
existing debt, unfavorable demographics and more modest growth.
For the U.S., between structurally higher mandatory outlays, the administration’s $1.5T
proposed defense package, and the 10-year U.S. Treasury yield sitting above 5%, spending
is likely headed higher. We continue to fade concerns of an impending U.S. debt crisis,
however, largely because economic growth continues to surprise to the upside. Expanding

output is good news for the debt-to-GDP ratio, which remains a touch below 100%.
Indeed, rather than being led higher on account of un-anchored inflation expectations, the
10-year U.S. Treasury yield has repriced to reflect better-than-expected nominal growth
(Exhibit 5A). Higher yields in the face of slowing growth, meanwhile, would be a different
story, not only for the fiscal outlook (worse) but also for U.S. equity valuations (lower).
Of course, after the U.S. accumulated another $2T deficit in FY 2026, 2 “rebuilding fiscal

space” in the IMF’s words will be politically unpopular and mathematically challenging.
In our view, the biggest near-term tail risk would be a surprise move lower in growth as
budget deficits at 6% of GDP in good times leave little room for bad times. This isn’t our
base case given robust consumer spending per our internal credit and debit card data and
ongoing investment in the AI buildout and beyond. We expect these two pillars to sustain
growth in 2027.

The other good news is that, contrary to concerns, demand for Treasurys remains robust.
Yields are attractive—that was the message from October’s successful $39B 10-year
Treasury note auction. At the same time, U.S. debt boasts an array of buyers: domestic
households, the Federal Reserve, mutual funds and exchange-traded funds, money market
funds, pension funds, foreign central banks, private investors overseas, and more. Per
Exhibit 5B, whereas foreign official investors once dominated some 40% of the U.S.

Treasury market during the 2008/2009 Great Financial Crisis, their share has fallen to
12% while domestic private investors now account for a majority of holdings. Translated:
demand is more diverse than most appreciate.

Investment Implications
In our view, higher nominal and real
yields present a compelling entry point
for investors seeking income and can
provide cushion in the face of higher
rate volatility. We expect demand for
U.S. assets to remain healthy and favor
the U.S. over developed economies in
Europe facing more modest growth and
fiscal instability.
